=== FILE: lib/features/file_manager/file_manager_screen.dart ===
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:share_plus/share_plus.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/i18n.dart';
import '../../services/app_state.dart';
import '../../services/storage/scan_store.dart';
import '../../widgets/tech_background.dart';

class FileManagerScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const FileManagerScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<FileManagerScreen> createState() => _FileManagerScreenState();
}

class _FileManagerScreenState extends State<FileManagerScreen> {
  final store = ScanStore.I;
  final s = AppState.I;
  String q = '';
  late Future<List<ScanEntry>> fut = store.list();

  void _refresh() => setState(() => fut = store.list(q: q));
  void _snack(String t) => ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(t)));
  String _two(int n) => n.toString().padLeft(2, '0');

  Future<String?> _askName(String title, String initial) {
    final c = TextEditingController(text: initial);
    return showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(title),
        content: TextField(controller: c, autofocus: true),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text(tr(ctx, 'Cancel', 'إلغاء'))),
          FilledButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, c.text.trim()), child: Text(tr(ctx, 'OK', 'موافق'))),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<bool> _confirmDelete(ScanEntry e) async {
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(tr(ctx, 'Delete scan?', 'حذف المسح؟')),
        content: Text(e.name),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: Text(tr(ctx, 'Cancel', 'إلغاء'))),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: TM.hot),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
            child: Text(tr(ctx, 'Delete', 'حذف')),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    return ok ?? false;
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    if (!s.grid.hasData) {
      _snack(tr(context, 'Nothing to save yet', 'لا توجد بيانات للحفظ'));
      return;
    }
    final name = await _askName(tr(context, 'Save current scan', 'حفظ المسح الحالي'), await store.nextName());
    if (name == null || name.isEmpty) return;
    await store.save(name, s.grid);
    _refresh();
  }

  Future<void> _import() async {
    try {
      final res = await FilePicker.platform.pickFiles(type: FileType.any);
      final path = res?.files.single.path;
      if (path == null) return;
      final raw = await ScanStore.readMatrixFile(path);
      final m = raw; // NaN cells stay "not measured"
      s.grid.loadMatrix(m);
      await store.save(p.basenameWithoutExtension(path), s.grid);
      _refresh();
      _snack(tr(context, 'Scan imported', 'تم استيراد المسح'));
    } catch (e) {
      _snack('Import failed: $e');
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: _save,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.save),
        label: Text(tr(context, 'Save scan', 'حفظ المسح')),
      ),
      body: TechBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Column(children: [
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 8),
              child: Row(children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: TextField(
                    onChanged: (v) {
                      q = v;
                      _refresh();
                    },
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
                      hintText: tr(context, 'Search scans…', 'ابحث في المسوحات…'),
                      filled: true,
                      fillColor: TM.navySurface,
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                FilledButton.icon(
                  onPressed: _import,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.upload_file),
                  label: Text(tr(context, 'Import', 'استيراد')),
                ),
              ]),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: FutureBuilder<List<ScanEntry>>(
                future: fut,
                builder: (_, snap) {
                  final list = snap.data ?? const <ScanEntry>[];
                  if (snap.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting && list.isEmpty) {
                    return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                  }
                  if (list.isEmpty) {
                    return Center(
                      child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                        const Icon(Icons.folder_open, size: 72, color: TM.grey),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        Text(tr(context, 'No saved scans', 'لا توجد مسوحات محفوظة'), style: const TextStyle(color: TM.grey)),
                      ]),
                    );
                  }
                  return ListView.builder(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 4, 12, 90),
                    itemCount: list.length,
                    itemBuilder: (_, i) => _card(list[i]),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ]),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _card(ScanEntry e) {
    final d = e.created;
    return Dismissible(
      key: ValueKey(e.id),
      direction: DismissDirection.endToStart,
      confirmDismiss: (_) => _confirmDelete(e),
      onDismissed: (_) async {
        await store.delete(e);
        _refresh();
      },
      background: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: TM.hot, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerEnd,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
        child: const Icon(Icons.delete, color: Colors.white),
      ),
      child: Card(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
        child: InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          onTap: () async {
            await store.load(e, s.grid);
            s.tab.value = 2;
          },
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 8, 4),
            child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              Row(children: [
                const Icon(Icons.insert_drive_file, color: TM.cyan),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(child: Text('${e.name}.json', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16))),
              ]),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(
                '${d.year}-${_two(d.month)}-${_two(d.day)}  ${_two(d.hour)}:${_two(d.minute)}'
                '   •   ${e.rows}×${e.cols}   •   ${e.spacingCm} cm',
                style: const TextStyle(color: TM.grey, fontSize: 12),
              ),
              Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end, children: [
                IconButton(
                  tooltip: tr(context, 'Rename', 'إعادة تسمية'),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.edit, size: 20),
                  onPressed: () async {
                    final n = await _askName(tr(context, 'Rename', 'إعادة تسمية'), e.name);
                    if (n != null && n.isNotEmpty) {
                      await store.rename(e, n);
                      _refresh();
                    }
                  },
                ),
                IconButton(
                  tooltip: tr(context, 'Export to CSV', 'تصدير CSV'),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.table_view, size: 20),
                  onPressed: () async {
                    final out = await store.exportCsv(e);
                    _snack('${tr(context, 'Saved', 'تم الحفظ')}: ${p.basename(out)}');
                  },
                ),
                IconButton(
                  tooltip: tr(context, 'Share', 'مشاركة'),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.share, size: 20),
                  onPressed: () async {
                    final out = await store.exportCsv(e);
                    await Share.shareXFiles([XFile(out), XFile(e.path)], text: e.name);
                  },
                ),
                IconButton(
                  tooltip: tr(context, 'Delete', 'حذف'),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.delete, size: 20, color: TM.hot),
                  onPressed: () async {
                    if (await _confirmDelete(e)) {
                      await store.delete(e);
                      _refresh();
                    }
                  },
                ),
              ]),
            ]),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/i18n.dart';
import '../../services/app_state.dart';

/// Grid Setup modal: start point, pattern, grid size, field dimensions.
Future<void> showGridSetup(BuildContext context) {
  final g = AppState.I.grid;
  var right = g.startRight;
  var zig = g.snake;
  var sp = g.spacingCm;
  var rows = g.rows, cols = g.cols;

  return showDialog(
    context: context,
    builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(builder: (ctx, set) {
      Widget label(String t) => Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 14, bottom: 6),
            child: Text(t, style: const TextStyle(color: TM.grey, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
          );
      Widget stepper(String name, int v, void Function(int) on) => Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: Text(name)),
            IconButton(onPressed: v > 4 ? () => set(() => on(v - 1)) : null, icon: const Icon(Icons.remove_circle_outline)),
            SizedBox(width: 36, child: Center(child: Text('$v', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))),
            IconButton(onPressed: v < 64 ? () => set(() => on(v + 1)) : null, icon: const Icon(Icons.add_circle_outline)),
          ]);

      return Dialog(
        backgroundColor: TM.navySurface,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20), side: const BorderSide(color: TM.cyan)),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              Row(children: [
                const Icon(Icons.grid_on, color: TM.cyan),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(tr(ctx, 'Grid Setup', 'إعداد الشبكة'), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              ]),
              label(tr(ctx, 'Start Point', 'نقطة البداية')),
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                child: SegmentedButton<bool>(
                  segments: [
                    ButtonSegment(value: false, label: Text(tr(ctx, 'Left', 'يسار'))),
                    ButtonSegment(value: true, label: Text(tr(ctx, 'Right', 'يمين'))),
                  ],
                  selected: {right},
                  onSelectionChanged: (v) => set(() => right = v.first),
                ),
              ),
              label(tr(ctx, 'Pattern', 'النمط')),
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                child: SegmentedButton<bool>(
                  segments: [
                    ButtonSegment(value: false, label: Text(tr(ctx, 'Parallel', 'متوازٍ'))),
                    ButtonSegment(value: true, label: Text(tr(ctx, 'Zigzag', 'متعرّج'))),
                  ],
                  selected: {zig},
                  onSelectionChanged: (v) => set(() => zig = v.first),
                ),
              ),
              label(tr(ctx, 'Grid Size', 'المسافة بين النقاط')),
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                child: SegmentedButton<int>(
                  segments: const [
                    ButtonSegment(value: 30, label: Text('30 cm')),
                    ButtonSegment(value: 40, label: Text('40 cm')),
                  ],
                  selected: {sp},
                  onSelectionChanged: (v) => set(() => sp = v.first),
                ),
              ),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
                child: Text(
                  sp == 30
                      ? tr(ctx, '30 cm: denser grid, better for small and medium targets.', '30 سم: شبكة أكثف، أفضل للأهداف الصغيرة والمتوسطة.')
                      : tr(ctx, '40 cm: fewer points, faster, suited to large targets.', '40 سم: نقاط أقل وأسرع، مناسبة للأهداف الكبيرة.'),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: TM.grey, fontSize: 12),
                ),
              ),
              label(tr(ctx, 'Points', 'عدد النقاط')),
              stepper(tr(ctx, 'Columns', 'أعمدة'), cols, (v) => cols = v),
              stepper(tr(ctx, 'Rows', 'صفوف'), rows, (v) => rows = v),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Container(
                width: double.infinity,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: TM.gold.withOpacity(0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(color: TM.gold),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  '${tr(ctx, 'Field Size', 'مساحة الحقل')}: '
                  '${(cols * sp / 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}m × ${(rows * sp / 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}m',
                  style: const TextStyle(color: TM.gold, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(tr(ctx, 'Applying clears the current scan.', 'التطبيق يمسح المسح الحالي.'),
                  style: const TextStyle(color: TM.grey, fontSize: 12)),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end, children: [
                TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text(tr(ctx, 'Cancel', 'إلغاء'))),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                FilledButton(
                  onPressed: () {
                    g.configure(rows: rows, cols: cols, spacingCm: sp, snake: zig, startRight: right);
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                  },
                  child: Text(tr(ctx, 'Apply', 'تطبيق')),
                ),
              ]),
            ]),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }),
  );
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../core/utils/i18n.dart';
import '../../services/app_state.dart';
import '../../services/ble/ble_service.dart';
import '../../widgets/compass_widget.dart';
import '../../widgets/grid_mini_map.dart';
import '../../widgets/tech_background.dart';
import '../../widgets/thermal_bar.dart';
import '../../widgets/trace_chart.dart';
import 'grid_setup_dialog.dart';

class LiveScanScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const LiveScanScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = AppState.I;
    return Scaffold(
      body: TechBackground(
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Column(children: [
            _TopBar(s),
            Expanded(
              child: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 8), children: [
                _LiveSection(s),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                const TraceChart(height: 80),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                _RecordCard(s),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                GridMiniMap(grid: s.grid, height: 220),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                _ScanParams(s),
                _ThresholdCard(s),
              ]),
            ),
            _Actions(s),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
          ]),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

class _TopBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppState s;
  const _TopBar(this.s);
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 0),
        child: Row(children: [
          StreamBuilder<BleStatus>(
            stream: s.ble.status,
            initialData: s.ble.current,
            builder: (_, snap) {
              final ok = snap.data == BleStatus.connected;
              return Chip(
                avatar: Icon(Icons.bluetooth, size: 18, color: ok ? TM.cyan : TM.grey),
                label: Text(ok ? tr(context, 'BLE Connected', 'متصل') : tr(context, 'Disconnected', 'غير متصل')),
                side: BorderSide(color: ok ? TM.cyan : Colors.white24),
              );
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          ValueListenableBuilder<int>(
            valueListenable: s.ble.battery,
            builder: (_, b, __) => Row(children: [
              Icon(b < 0 ? Icons.battery_unknown : (b < 20 ? Icons.battery_alert : Icons.battery_full), size: 20,
                  color: b >= 0 && b < 20 ? TM.hot : Colors.white),
              Text(b < 0 ? ' --' : ' $b%'),
            ]),
          ),
          const Spacer(),
          const CompassWidget(size: 54),
        ]),
      );
}

class _LiveSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppState s;
  const _LiveSection(this.s);
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => ListenableBuilder(
        listenable: Listenable.merge([s.live, s.baseline, s.thresholdNt, s.ntPerCount, s.peak]),
        builder: (_, __) {
          final v = s.live.value;
          final st = s.alertState(v);
          final alert = st != 0;
          final d = s.deltaNt(v);
          final vec = s.ble.vectorSeen; // sensor sends nT (x,y,z)
          final thr = s.thresholdNt.value;
          final fraction = vec ? 0.5 + d / (8 * thr) : v / 1023;
          final ticks = vec
              ? ['+${(4 * thr).round()}', '+${(2 * thr).round()}', '0', '-${(2 * thr).round()}', '-${(4 * thr).round()}']
              : ['1023', '768', '512', '256', '0'];
          return SizedBox(
            height: 300,
            child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch, children: [
              Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(4, 8, 8, 8), child: ThermalBar(fraction: fraction, ticks: ticks)),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [
                  FittedBox(child: Text('${v.round()}', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.displayLarge)),
                  Text(
                    s.baseline.value == null
                        ? tr(context, 'Not calibrated', 'غير معايَر')
                        : 'Δ ${d >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.toStringAsFixed(0)} nT',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, color: alert ? TM.gold : TM.grey, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  AnimatedContainer(
                    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 150),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 6),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: st > 0 ? TM.gold : (st < 0 ? TM.cyan : Colors.transparent),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                      border: Border.all(color: alert ? (st > 0 ? TM.gold : TM.cyan) : Colors.white24),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      st > 0 ? tr(context, 'METAL', 'معدن') : (st < 0 ? tr(context, 'VOID', 'فراغ') : tr(context, 'Normal', 'طبيعي')),
                      style: TextStyle(color: alert ? TM.navy : TM.grey, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [
                    Text('${tr(context, 'Peak', 'الذروة')}: ${s.peak.value.toStringAsFixed(0)} nT',
                        style: const TextStyle(color: TM.grey, fontSize: 13)),
                    IconButton(
                      visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
                      tooltip: tr(context, 'Reset peak', 'تصفير الذروة'),
                      onPressed: s.resetPeak,
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.restart_alt, size: 20, color: TM.cyan),
                    ),
                  ]),
                ]),
              ),
            ]),
          );
        },
      );
}

class _RecordCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppState s;
  const _RecordCard(this.s);
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => ListenableBuilder(
        listenable: s.grid,
        builder: (_, __) {
          final g = s.grid;
          final (nr, nc) = g.nextCell;
          return Card(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              child: Column(children: [
                Row(children: [
                  Text('${tr(context, 'Point', 'نقطة')} ${g.captured} / ${g.total}', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const Spacer(),
                  Text(
                    g.isComplete
                        ? tr(context, 'Scan complete', 'اكتمل المسح')
                        : '${tr(context, 'Line', 'خط')} ${g.currentLine}/${g.rows} • ${tr(context, 'next', 'التالي')}: ${nr + 1},${nc + 1}',
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: TM.grey),
                  ),
                ]),
                Text('${g.rows}×${g.cols} • ${g.spacingCm} cm • ${g.fieldWidthM.toStringAsFixed(1)}×${g.fieldHeightM.toStringAsFixed(1)} m',
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: TM.grey)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                LinearProgressIndicator(value: g.total == 0 ? 0 : g.captured / g.total, color: TM.cyan, backgroundColor: Colors.white12),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Row(children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: FilledButton.icon(
                      style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: TM.gold, foregroundColor: TM.navy),
                      onPressed: g.isComplete ? null : s.capture,
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.add_location_alt),
                      label: Text(tr(context, 'RECORD', 'تسجيل'), style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    ),
                  ),
                  IconButton(tooltip: tr(context, 'Undo last point', 'تراجع عن آخر نقطة'), onPressed: g.captured == 0 ? null : g.undoLast, icon: const Icon(Icons.undo, color: TM.cyan)),
                  IconButton(tooltip: tr(context, 'Grid Setup', 'إعداد الشبكة'), onPressed: () => showGridSetup(context), icon: const Icon(Icons.grid_on, color: TM.cyan)),
                  IconButton(
                    tooltip: tr(context, 'New scan', 'مسح جديد'),
                    onPressed: () async {
                      final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
                        context: context,
                        builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                          title: Text(tr(ctx, 'Clear the current scan?', 'مسح بيانات المسح الحالي؟')),
                          actions: [
                            TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: Text(tr(ctx, 'Cancel', 'إلغاء'))),
                            FilledButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true), child: Text(tr(ctx, 'Clear', 'مسح'))),
                          ],
                        ),
                      );
                      if (ok == true) g.reset();
                    },
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_sweep, color: TM.grey),
                  ),
                ]),
              ]),
            ),
          );
        },
      );
}

=== FILE: lib/services/app_state.dart ===
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import '../core/processing/pipeline.dart';
import '../features/analysis_3d/grid_processor.dart';
import '../features/analysis_3d/palettes.dart';
import 'ble/ble_service.dart';
import 'tone_player.dart';
import 'trace_buffer.dart';

/// App-wide singleton. Only the locale uses ChangeNotifier (root rebuild);
/// everything that changes often uses ValueNotifier to keep rebuilds local.
class AppState extends ChangeNotifier {
  AppState._() {
    ble.samples.listen(_onSample);
    grid.addListener(_persist);
    _restore();
  }
  static final I = AppState._();

  final ble = BleService();
  final grid = GridProcessor(rows: 16, cols: 16, spacingCm: 30);
  final tone = TonePlayer();
  final trace = TraceBuffer();

  Locale locale = const Locale('en');
  void toggleLocale() {
    locale = locale.languageCode == 'en' ? const Locale('ar') : const Locale('en');
    notifyListeners();
  }

  // ---- navigation / live values -------------------------------------------
  final tab = ValueNotifier<int>(0);
  final live = ValueNotifier<double>(0);
  final baseline = ValueNotifier<double?>(null);
  final peak = ValueNotifier<double>(0); // largest |delta| (nT) since the last reset
  final spread = ValueNotifier<double>(0); // steadiness: spread of the last readings (nT)

  // ---- scan settings ----------------------------------------------------
  final thresholdNt = ValueNotifier<double>(50); // 15..200
  final ntPerCount = ValueNotifier<double>(1.0); // sensor scale factor
  final audioOn = ValueNotifier<bool>(true);
  final vibrateOn = ValueNotifier<bool>(true);
  final simulate = ValueNotifier<bool>(false);
  final pulses = ValueNotifier<int>(3); // readings averaged per point (1..10)
  final smartCapture = ValueNotifier<bool>(false); // record automatically when steady
  final stabNt = ValueNotifier<double>(15); // steadiness limit (nT)
  final dwellSec = ValueNotifier<double>(2); // time the reading must stay steady (s)

  // ---- analysis settings ------------------------------------------------
  final soil = ValueNotifier<int>(0); // index into soilTypes
  final refDepthCm = ValueNotifier<double>(0); // depth calibration (0 = off)
  final refAmpNt = ValueNotifier<double>(0);
  final steps = ValueNotifier<List<ProcStep>>(const []); // filter history
  final palette = ValueNotifier<PaletteId>(PaletteId.magnetic);
  final reversePalette = ValueNotifier<bool>(false);
  final reference = ValueNotifier<List<List<double>>?>(null); // for "Compare"

  void addStep(ProcStep s) => steps.value = [...steps.value, s];
  void undoStep() {
    if (steps.value.isNotEmpty) steps.value = steps.value.sublist(0, steps.value.length - 1);
  }

  void clearSteps() => steps.value = const [];

  // ---- live processing --------------------------------------------------
  final List<double> _recent = [];
  DateTime _lastBeep = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
  DateTime? _steadySince;
  DateTime _lastCapture = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
  bool _needMove = false;

  double deltaNt(double v) => baseline.value == null ? 0 : (v - baseline.value!) * ntPerCount.value;

  /// +1 = positive anomaly (metal), -1 = negative anomaly (void), 0 = normal.
  int alertState(double v) {
    if (baseline.value == null) return 0;
    final d = deltaNt(v);
    if (d > thresholdNt.value) return 1;
    if (d < -thresholdNt.value) return -1;
    return 0;
  }

  bool isAlert(double v) => alertState(v) != 0;

  void _onSample(SensorSample s) {
    final v = s.value;
    live.value = v;
    trace.add(v);
    _recent.add(v);
    if (_recent.length > 60) _recent.removeAt(0);

    final n = math.max(1, pulses.value);
    final win = _recent.length > n ? _recent.sublist(_recent.length - n) : _recent;
    final sp = (win.reduce(math.max) - win.reduce(math.min)) * ntPerCount.value;
    spread.value = sp;

    if (baseline.value != null) {
      final p = deltaNt(v).abs();
      if (p > peak.value) peak.value = p;
    }

    final st = alertState(v);
    final now = DateTime.now();
    if (st != 0 && now.difference(_lastBeep).inMilliseconds > 350) {
      _lastBeep = now;
      if (audioOn.value) st > 0 ? tone.metal() : tone.voidTone();
      if (vibrateOn.value) st > 0 ? HapticFeedback.heavyImpact() : HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
    }

    // smart capture: record when the reading has been steady long enough
    if (smartCapture.value && !grid.isComplete) {
      final steady = win.length >= n && sp <= stabNt.value;
      if (!steady) {
        _steadySince = null;
        _needMove = false; // the user moved: allow the next capture
      } else {
        _steadySince ??= now;
        if (now.difference(_lastCapture).inSeconds > 8) _needMove = false; // safety timeout
        final held = now.difference(_steadySince!).inMilliseconds / 1000;
        if (!_needMove && held >= dwellSec.value) {
          capture();
          _needMove = true;
          _steadySince = null;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /// Records the average of the last [pulses] readings at the next grid cell.
  void capture() {
    if (grid.isComplete) return;
    final n = math.max(1, pulses.value);
    final win = _recent.length > n ? _recent.sublist(_recent.length - n) : _recent;
    final v = win.isEmpty ? live.value : win.reduce((a, b) => a + b) / win.length;
    grid.addReading(v);
    _lastCapture = DateTime.now();
    if (vibrateOn.value) HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
  }

  void zeroPoint() => baseline.value = live.value;
  void resetPeak() => peak.value = 0;

  /// Smart calibration: average ~2 s of readings and use it as the baseline.
  Future<double?> calibrate({Duration duration = const Duration(seconds: 2)}) async {
    final vals = <double>[];
    final sub = ble.samples.listen((s) => vals.add(s.value));
    await Future.delayed(duration);
    await sub.cancel();
    if (vals.isEmpty) return null;
    baseline.value = vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b) / vals.length;
    peak.value = 0;
    return baseline.value;
  }

  // ---- sensor simulator (test the UI without hardware) ------------------
  Timer? _sim;
  double _t = 0;
  void setSimulate(bool on) {
    simulate.value = on;
    _sim?.cancel();
    if (!on) return;
    final rnd = math.Random();
    _sim = Timer.periodic(const Duration(milliseconds: 100), (_) {
      _t += 0.1;
      final ph = _t % 20;
      final bump = 250 * math.exp(-math.pow(ph - 9.5, 2) / 0.6);
      final v = 500 + 25 * math.sin(_t * 0.7) + rnd.nextInt(9) - 4 + bump;
      ble.injectSample(v.clamp(0, 1023).toDouble());
    });
  }

  // ---- the current scan survives app restarts / crashes ----------------
  Timer? _saveT;
  Future<File> _curFile() async =>
      File('${(await getApplicationSupportDirectory()).path}/current_scan.json');

  void _persist() {
    _saveT?.cancel();
    _saveT = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 500), () async {
      try {
        await (await _curFile()).writeAsString(jsonEncode(grid.toJson('current')));
      } catch (_) {}
    });
  }

  Future<void> _restore() async {
    try {
      final f = await _curFile();
      if (!await f.exists()) return;
      final j = jsonDecode(await f.readAsString()) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final m = [
        for (final r in j['matrix'] as List)
          [for (final v in r as List) v == null ? double.nan : (v as num).toDouble()]
      ];
      grid.snake = j['snake'] as bool? ?? grid.snake;
      grid.startRight = j['start_right'] as bool? ?? grid.startRight;
      grid.loadMatrix(m, spacing: j['spacing_cm'] as int?, cursor: j['cursor'] as int?);
    } catch (_) {}
  }
}
=== FILE: lib/services/ble/ble_service.dart ===
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_blue_plus/flutter_blue_plus.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';

class SensorSample {
  final double value; // counts (0..1023) or nT magnitude when the sensor sends x,y,z
  final DateTime t;
  SensorSample(this.value, this.t);
}

enum BleStatus { disconnected, connecting, connected, reconnecting }

/// BLE link to the ESP32 gradiometer.
/// Protocol: ASCII lines, either one number per line (e.g. 512) or "x,y,z" (nT; the field
/// magnitude is used). Optional "BAT:87" lines report the battery.
class BleService {
  // Nordic UART defaults. If your firmware uses other UUIDs, the service falls back
  // to the first characteristic that supports notifications.
  static final Guid txCharUuid = Guid('6E400003-B5A3-F393-E0A9-E50E24DCCA9E');

  final _samples = StreamController<SensorSample>.broadcast();
  final _status = StreamController<BleStatus>.broadcast();
  Stream<SensorSample> get samples => _samples.stream;
  Stream<BleStatus> get status => _status.stream;

  BleStatus current = BleStatus.disconnected;
  final ValueNotifier<int> battery = ValueNotifier<int>(-1);
  bool vectorSeen = false; // true once a 'x,y,z' line was received (values are nT)
  String? connectedName;
  String? lastName;

  BluetoothDevice? _device;
  StreamSubscription? _connSub, _dataSub;
  String _buffer = '';
  bool _wantConnected = false;
  int _retry = 0;

  void _emit(BleStatus s) {
    current = s;
    _status.add(s);
  }

  /// Used by the built-in sensor simulator.
  void injectSample(double v) => _samples.add(SensorSample(v, DateTime.now()));

  // ---- scanning --------------------------------------------------------
  Stream<List<ScanResult>> get results => FlutterBluePlus.scanResults;
  Stream<bool> get scanning => FlutterBluePlus.isScanning;
  Future<void> startScan() =>
      FlutterBluePlus.startScan(timeout: const Duration(seconds: 8));

  // ---- connect / auto-reconnect ---------------------------------------
  Future<void> connect(BluetoothDevice device) async {
    _wantConnected = true;
    _device = device;
    connectedName = device.platformName.isEmpty ? device.remoteId.str : device.platformName;
    lastName = connectedName;
    await _saveLastDevice(device.remoteId.str, connectedName!);
    await _openLink();
  }

  Future<void> autoConnectLast() async {
    final info = await lastDevice();
    if (info == null) return;
    lastName = info['name'];
    await connect(BluetoothDevice.fromId(info['id']!));
  }

  Future<void> _openLink() async {
    final d = _device;
    if (d == null) return;
    try {
      _emit(BleStatus.connecting);
      await FlutterBluePlus.stopScan();
      await d.connect(timeout: const Duration(seconds: 10), autoConnect: false);

      await _connSub?.cancel();
      _connSub = d.connectionState.listen((s) {
        if (s == BluetoothConnectionState.disconnected && _wantConnected) {
          _emit(BleStatus.reconnecting);
          _scheduleReconnect();
        }
      });

      final services = await d.discoverServices();
      BluetoothCharacteristic? ch;
      for (final s in services) {
        for (final c in s.characteristics) {
          if (c.uuid == txCharUuid) ch = c;
        }
      }
      ch ??= services
          .expand((s) => s.characteristics)
          .where((c) => c.properties.notify)
          .firstOrNull;
      if (ch == null) throw Exception('No notify characteristic');
      await ch.setNotifyValue(true);

      await _dataSub?.cancel();
      _dataSub = ch.onValueReceived.listen(_onBytes);
      d.cancelWhenDisconnected(_dataSub!);

      _retry = 0;
      _buffer = '';
      _emit(BleStatus.connected);
    } catch (_) {
      _emit(BleStatus.reconnecting);
      _scheduleReconnect();
    }
  }

  void _scheduleReconnect() {
    if (!_wantConnected) return;
    final delay = Duration(seconds: (1 << _retry.clamp(0, 5)));
    _retry++;
    Timer(delay, () {
      if (_wantConnected) _openLink();
    });
  }

  Future<void> disconnect() async {
    _wantConnected = false;
    await _dataSub?.cancel();
    await _connSub?.cancel();
    await _device?.disconnect();
    _emit(BleStatus.disconnected);
  }

  // ---- parsing ---------------------------------------------------------
  void _onBytes(List<int> bytes) {
    _buffer += utf8.decode(bytes, allowMalformed: true);
    int nl;
    while ((nl = _buffer.indexOf('\n')) >= 0) {
      final line = _buffer.substring(0, nl).trim();
      _buffer = _buffer.substring(nl + 1);
      if (line.isEmpty) continue;
      if (line.startsWith('BAT:')) {
        battery.value = int.tryParse(line.substring(4)) ?? battery.value;
        continue;
      }
      final parts = line.split(RegExp(r'[,;\s]+')).where((p) => p.isNotEmpty).toList();
      if (parts.length == 3) {
        final x = double.tryParse(parts[0]), y = double.tryParse(parts[1]), z = double.tryParse(parts[2]);
        if (x != null && y != null && z != null) {
          vectorSeen = true;
          injectSample(math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z));
        }
      } else if (parts.length == 1) {
        final v = double.tryParse(parts[0]);
        if (v != null && v.isFinite) injectSample(v);
      }
    }
  }

  // ---- last device (JSON file) ----------------------------------------
  Future<File> _cfg() async =>
      File('${(await getApplicationSupportDirectory()).path}/last_device.json');
  Future<void> _saveLastDevice(String id, String name) async =>
      (await _cfg()).writeAsString(jsonEncode({'id': id, 'name': name}));
  Future<Map<String, String>?> lastDevice() async {
    try {
      final f = await _cfg();
      if (!await f.exists()) return null;
      final m = jsonDecode(await f.readAsString()) as Map;
      if (m['id'] == null) return null;
      return {'id': m['id'] as String, 'name': (m['name'] ?? m['id']) as String};
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }
}
=== FILE: lib/services/storage/scan_store.dart ===
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:sqflite/sqflite.dart';
import '../../features/analysis_3d/grid_processor.dart';

class ScanEntry {
  final int id;
  final String name, path;
  final DateTime created;
  final int rows, cols, spacingCm;
  ScanEntry(this.id, this.name, this.path, this.created, this.rows, this.cols, this.spacingCm);
}

/// SQLite index (table `scans`) + one JSON file per scan.
class ScanStore {
  ScanStore._();
  static final ScanStore I = ScanStore._();
  Database? _db;

  Future<Database> get db async => _db ??= await openDatabase(
        p.join(await getDatabasesPath(), 'terramag.db'),
        version: 1,
        onCreate: (d, _) => d.execute(
            'CREATE TABLE scans(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT, path TEXT, '
            'created_at TEXT, rows INTEGER, cols INTEGER, spacing_cm INTEGER)'),
      );

  Future<Directory> _dir(String sub) async {
    final d = Directory(p.join((await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory()).path, sub));
    if (!await d.exists()) await d.create(recursive: true);
    return d;
  }

  String _safe(String n) => n.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^A-Za-z0-9_\-\u0600-\u06FF]'), '_');

  Future<String> nextName() async {
    final n = Sqflite.firstIntValue(await (await db).rawQuery('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM scans')) ?? 0;
    return 'Scan_${(n + 1).toString().padLeft(2, '0')}_Target';
  }

  Future<int> save(String name, GridProcessor g) async {
    var path = p.join((await _dir('scans')).path, '${_safe(name)}.json');
    if (await File(path).exists()) {
      path = p.join((await _dir('scans')).path,
          '${_safe(name)}_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}.json');
    }
    await File(path).writeAsString(jsonEncode(g.toJson(name)));
    return (await db).insert('scans', {
      'name': name,
      'path': path,
      'created_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      'rows': g.rows,
      'cols': g.cols,
      'spacing_cm': g.spacingCm,
    });
  }

  Future<List<ScanEntry>> list({String q = ''}) async {
    final rows = await (await db).query('scans',
        where: q.isEmpty ? null : 'name LIKE ?',
        whereArgs: q.isEmpty ? null : ['%$q%'],
        orderBy: 'id DESC');
    return [
      for (final r in rows)
        ScanEntry(r['id'] as int, r['name'] as String, r['path'] as String,
            DateTime.parse(r['created_at'] as String), r['rows'] as int, r['cols'] as int,
            r['spacing_cm'] as int)
    ];
  }

  Future<void> rename(ScanEntry e, String newName) async {
    final newPath = p.join(p.dirname(e.path), '${_safe(newName)}.json');
    var path = e.path;
    if (await File(e.path).exists() && !await File(newPath).exists()) {
      await File(e.path).rename(newPath);
      path = newPath;
    }
    await (await db).update('scans', {'name': newName, 'path': path},
        where: 'id = ?', whereArgs: [e.id]);
  }

  Future<void> delete(ScanEntry e) async {
    final f = File(e.path);
    if (await f.exists()) await f.delete();
    await (await db).delete('scans', where: 'id = ?', whereArgs: [e.id]);
  }

  Future<void> load(ScanEntry e, GridProcessor g) async {
    final j = jsonDecode(await File(e.path).readAsString()) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    final m = [
      for (final r in j['matrix'] as List)
        [for (final v in r as List) v == null ? double.nan : (v as num).toDouble()]
    ];
    g.snake = j['snake'] as bool? ?? g.snake;
    g.startRight = j['start_right'] as bool? ?? g.startRight;
    g.loadMatrix(m, spacing: j['spacing_cm'] as int?);
  }

  /// CSV: one matrix row per line, empty field = not measured.
  Future<String> exportCsv(ScanEntry e) async {
    final g = GridProcessor(rows: 1, cols: 1);
    await load(e, g);
    final sb = StringBuffer('# TerraMag spacing_cm=${g.spacingCm} rows=${g.rows} cols=${g.cols}\n');
    for (final r in g.matrix) {
      sb.writeln(r.map((v) => v.isNaN ? '' : v.toStringAsFixed(1)).join(','));
    }
    final out = p.join((await _dir('exports')).path, '${_safe(e.name)}.csv');
    await File(out).writeAsString(sb.toString());
    return out;
  }

  /// Matrix CSV (one grid row per line, empty field = not measured) or "x,y,value" triples.
  static List<List<double>> parseCsv(String text) {
    final rows = <List<double>>[];
    for (final line in const LineSplitter().convert(text)) {
      final t = line.trim();
      if (t.isEmpty || t.startsWith('#')) continue;
      rows.add(t.split(RegExp(r'[,;\t]')).map((s) => double.tryParse(s.trim()) ?? double.nan).toList());
    }
    if (rows.isEmpty) throw const FormatException('Empty CSV');

    // x,y,value triples: integer indices whose extent matches the number of lines
    if (rows.length >= 4 && rows.every((r) => r.length == 3 && !r[0].isNaN && !r[1].isNaN)) {
      final ints = rows.every((r) => r[0] == r[0].roundToDouble() && r[1] == r[1].roundToDouble() && r[0] >= 0 && r[1] >= 0);
      if (ints) {
        final w = rows.map((r) => r[0].toInt()).reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b) + 1;
        final h = rows.map((r) => r[1].toInt()).reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b) + 1;
        if (w * h == rows.length) {
          final g = List.generate(h, (_) => List.filled(w, double.nan));
          for (final r in rows) {
            g[r[1].toInt()][r[0].toInt()] = r[2];
          }
          return g;
        }
      }
    }
    final w = rows.map((r) => r.length).reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b);
    return [for (final r in rows) [...r, ...List.filled(w - r.length, double.nan)]];
  }

  /// Reads a scan file: our JSON format, or CSV.
  static Future<List<List<double>>> readMatrixFile(String path) async {
    final text = await File(path).readAsString();
    if (path.toLowerCase().endsWith('.json')) {
      final j = jsonDecode(text) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      return [
        for (final r in j['matrix'] as List)
          [for (final v in r as List) v == null ? double.nan : (v as num).toDouble()]
      ];
    }
    return parseCsv(text);
  }
}
=== FILE: lib/services/tone_player.dart ===
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'package:audioplayers/audioplayers.dart';

/// Synthesised alert tones (no audio assets needed):
/// metal = high double beep, void = low long tone.
class TonePlayer {
  final AudioPlayer _p = AudioPlayer();
  late final Uint8List _metal = _wav([[1500, 0.09], [0, 0.05], [1500, 0.09]]);
  late final Uint8List _void = _wav([[320, 0.30]]);

  Future<void> metal() => _play(_metal);
  Future<void> voidTone() => _play(_void);

  Future<void> _play(Uint8List b) async {
    try {
      await _p.stop();
      await _p.play(BytesSource(b));
    } catch (_) {/* audio is best-effort */}
  }

  /// segments: [frequencyHz (0 = silence), seconds]. 16-bit mono PCM WAV, 22.05 kHz.
  static Uint8List _wav(List<List<double>> segs) {
    const rate = 22050;
    final samples = <int>[];
    for (final s in segs) {
      final n = (s[1] * rate).round();
      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        final fade = math.min(1.0, math.min(i, n - i) / (rate * 0.008)); // click-free edges
        final v = s[0] == 0 ? 0.0 : math.sin(2 * math.pi * s[0] * i / rate) * 0.8 * fade;
        samples.add((v * 32767).round());
      }
    }
    final data = ByteData(44 + samples.length * 2);
    void str(int o, String t) {
      for (var i = 0; i < t.length; i++) {
        data.setUint8(o + i, t.codeUnitAt(i));
      }
    }

    str(0, 'RIFF');
    data.setUint32(4, 36 + samples.length * 2, Endian.little);
    str(8, 'WAVE');
    str(12, 'fmt ');
    data.setUint32(16, 16, Endian.little);
    data.setUint16(20, 1, Endian.little);
    data.setUint16(22, 1, Endian.little);
    data.setUint32(24, rate, Endian.little);
    data.setUint32(28, rate * 2, Endian.little);
    data.setUint16(32, 2, Endian.little);
    data.setUint16(34, 16, Endian.little);
    str(36, 'data');
    data.setUint32(40, samples.length * 2, Endian.little);
    for (var i = 0; i < samples.length; i++) {
      data.setInt16(44 + i * 2, samples[i], Endian.little);
    }
    return data.buffer.asUint8List();
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

/// Ring buffer of the latest readings for the live trace chart.
class TraceBuffer extends ChangeNotifier {
  static const maxLen = 150;
  final List<double> data = [];
  void add(double v) {
    data.add(v);
    if (data.length > maxLen) data.removeAt(0);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void clear() {
    data.clear();
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_compass/flutter_compass.dart';
import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Digital compass driven by the phone's magnetometer (works offline).
class CompassWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final double size;
  const CompassWidget({super.key, this.size = 56});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => StreamBuilder<CompassEvent>(
        stream: FlutterCompass.events,
        builder: (_, snap) {
          final h = snap.data?.heading;
          return SizedBox(
            width: size,
            height: size,
            child: CustomPaint(
              painter: _CompassPainter(h),
              child: Center(
                child: Text(h == null ? '--' : '${((h % 360 + 360) % 360).round()}°',
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      );
}

class _CompassPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final double? heading;
  _CompassPainter(this.heading);
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size s) {
    final c = s.center(Offset.zero);
    final r = s.width / 2 - 2;
    canvas.drawCircle(c, r, Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 2
      ..color = TM.cyan);
    canvas.save();
    canvas.translate(c.dx, c.dy);
    canvas.rotate(-(heading ?? 0) * math.pi / 180);
    final north = Path()
      ..moveTo(0, -r)
      ..lineTo(-5, -r + 10)
      ..lineTo(5, -r + 10)
      ..close();
    canvas.drawPath(north, Paint()..color = TM.hot);
    canvas.restore();
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_CompassPainter o) => o.heading != heading;
}

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../features/analysis_3d/grid_processor.dart';
import '../features/analysis_3d/palettes.dart';

/// Live map of the scan grid: measured cells are coloured, the next cell is outlined in gold.
class GridMiniMap extends StatelessWidget {
  final GridProcessor grid;
  final double height;
  const GridMiniMap({super.key, required this.grid, this.height = 220});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => ListenableBuilder(
        listenable: grid,
        builder: (_, __) => SizedBox(
          height: height,
          child: CustomPaint(size: Size.infinite, painter: _MapPainter(grid)),
        ),
      );

class _MapPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final GridProcessor g;
  _MapPainter(this.g);
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final rows = g.rows, cols = g.cols;
    final cell = math.min(size.width / cols, size.height / rows);
    final ox = (size.width - cell * cols) / 2, oy = (size.height - cell * rows) / 2;
    final scale = ColorScale(PaletteId.magnetic, false, g.stats);
    final fill = Paint(), line = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 0.6
      ..color = Colors.white24;
    for (var r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      for (var c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
        final rect = Rect.fromLTWH(ox + c * cell, oy + r * cell, cell, cell);
        final v = g.matrix[r][c];
        if (!v.isNaN) {
          fill.color = scale.colorOf(v);
          canvas.drawRect(rect, fill);
        }
        canvas.drawRect(rect, line);
      }
    }
    if (!g.isComplete) {
      final (r, c) = g.nextCell;
      canvas.drawRect(Rect.fromLTWH(ox + c * cell, oy + r * cell, cell, cell), Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 2.5
        ..color = TM.gold);
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _MapPainter old) => true;
}

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../features/analysis_3d/grid_processor.dart';
import '../features/analysis_3d/palettes.dart';

class HeatBox {
  final Rect rect; // cell units of the drawn matrix
  final String label;
  final Color color;
  const HeatBox(this.rect, this.label, this.color);
}

/// Flat 2-D heat-map with detection boxes and labels.
class HeatmapPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final bool rtl;
  final List<List<double>> m;
  final List<HeatBox> boxes;
  final PaletteId palette;
  final bool reverse;
  final ScanStats? statsOverride; // e.g. symmetric range for difference maps
  HeatmapPainter(this.rtl, this.m, this.boxes,
      {this.palette = PaletteId.magnetic, this.reverse = false, this.statsOverride});

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final rows = m.length, cols = m.first.length;
    final flat = m.expand((r) => r).toList();
    final stats = statsOverride ??
        ScanStats(flat.reduce(math.min), flat.reduce((a, b) => a + b) / flat.length, flat.reduce(math.max));
    final scale = ColorScale(palette, reverse, stats);
    final cell = math.min((size.width - 16) / cols, (size.height - 16) / rows);
    final ox = (size.width - cell * cols) / 2, oy = (size.height - cell * rows) / 2;
    final paint = Paint();
    for (var r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      for (var c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
        paint.color = scale.colorOf(m[r][c]);
        canvas.drawRect(Rect.fromLTWH(ox + c * cell, oy + r * cell, cell + 0.6, cell + 0.6), paint);
      }
    }
    for (final b in boxes) {
      final rc = Rect.fromLTRB(ox + b.rect.left * cell, oy + b.rect.top * cell, ox + b.rect.right * cell, oy + b.rect.bottom * cell);
      canvas.drawRect(rc, Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 2.5
        ..color = b.color);
      final tp = TextPainter(
        text: TextSpan(text: b.label, style: TextStyle(color: TM.navy, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, backgroundColor: b.color)),
        textDirection: rtl ? TextDirection.rtl : TextDirection.ltr,
      )..layout();
      tp.paint(canvas, Offset(rc.left, math.max(0, rc.top - tp.height)));
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant HeatmapPainter old) => true;
}

/// Cross-section line chart (one row or column) with the normal level and the threshold band.
class ProfilePainter extends CustomPainter {
  final List<double> values;
  final double normal, thr;
  ProfilePainter(this.values, this.normal, this.thr);

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    const pad = 28.0;
    final w = size.width - pad * 2, h = size.height - pad * 2;
    var lo = values.reduce(math.min), hi = values.reduce(math.max);
    lo = math.min(lo, normal - thr);
    hi = math.max(hi, normal + thr);
    final span = (hi - lo).abs() < 1e-9 ? 1.0 : hi - lo;
    double y(double v) => pad + h - (v - lo) / span * h;
    double x(int i) => pad + (values.length == 1 ? 0 : i / (values.length - 1)) * w;

    canvas.drawRect(Rect.fromLTRB(pad, y(normal + thr), pad + w, y(normal - thr)), Paint()..color = TM.cyan.withOpacity(0.10));
    canvas.drawLine(Offset(pad, y(normal)), Offset(pad + w, y(normal)), Paint()
      ..color = TM.grey
      ..strokeWidth = 1);
    final path = Path();
    for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
      final p = Offset(x(i), y(values[i]));
      i == 0 ? path.moveTo(p.dx, p.dy) : path.lineTo(p.dx, p.dy);
    }
    canvas.drawPath(path, Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 2.5
      ..color = TM.cyan);
    for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
      final out = (values[i] - normal).abs() > thr;
      canvas.drawCircle(Offset(x(i), y(values[i])), out ? 4.5 : 2.5, Paint()..color = out ? TM.gold : TM.cyan);
    }
    void label(String t, Offset o) {
      final tp = TextPainter(text: TextSpan(text: t, style: const TextStyle(color: TM.grey, fontSize: 10)), textDirection: TextDirection.ltr)..layout();
      tp.paint(canvas, o);
    }

    label('${hi.round()}', const Offset(2, pad - 6));
    label('${lo.round()}', Offset(2, pad + h - 6));
    label('normal ${normal.round()}', Offset(pad + 4, y(normal) - 13));
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant ProfilePainter old) => true;
}
=== FILE: lib/widgets/iso_grid_painter.dart ===
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../features/analysis_3d/grid_processor.dart';
import '../features/analysis_3d/palettes.dart';

/// Isometric voxel view. Auto-fits the canvas, rotates about the vertical axis,
/// hides layers, and outlines the AI detection in gold.
class IsoGridPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final List<List<double>> m; // already filtered, raw units
  final double depth; // 0.2 .. 2.0
  final bool volumetric;
  final double rotation; // radians
  final double elevation; // camera elevation in radians (~0.61 = isometric, 1.57 = top view)
  final Set<Layer> hidden;
  final Rect? highlight; // in cell units of [m]
  final PaletteId palette;
  final bool reverse;
  final bool wireframe;

  IsoGridPainter(this.m,
      {this.depth = 1,
      this.volumetric = false,
      this.rotation = 0.6,
      this.elevation = 0.61,
      this.hidden = const {},
      this.highlight,
      this.palette = PaletteId.magnetic,
      this.reverse = false,
      this.wireframe = false});

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final rows = m.length, cols = m.first.length;
    final flat = m.expand((r) => r).toList();
    final lo = flat.reduce(math.min), hi = flat.reduce(math.max);
    final stats = ScanStats(lo, flat.reduce((a, b) => a + b) / flat.length, hi);
    final span = (hi - lo).abs() < 1e-9 ? 1.0 : hi - lo;
    final colors = ColorScale(palette, reverse, stats);

    final cr = math.cos(rotation), sr = math.sin(rotation);
    final se = math.sin(elevation), ce = math.cos(elevation);
    Offset pu(double x, double y, double z) {
      final rx = x * cr - y * sr, ry = x * sr + y * cr;
      return Offset((rx - ry) * 0.7071, (rx + ry) * 0.7071 * se - z * ce);
    }

    final maxH = 5.0 * depth;
    var minX = 1e9, maxX = -1e9, minY = 1e9, maxY = -1e9;
    for (final x in [-cols / 2, cols / 2]) {
      for (final y in [-rows / 2, rows / 2]) {
        for (final z in [0.0, maxH]) {
          final p = pu(x, y, z);
          minX = math.min(minX, p.dx);
          maxX = math.max(maxX, p.dx);
          minY = math.min(minY, p.dy);
          maxY = math.max(maxY, p.dy);
        }
      }
    }
    final scale = math.min(size.width * 0.94 / (maxX - minX), size.height * 0.92 / (maxY - minY));
    final bc = Offset((minX + maxX) / 2, (minY + maxY) / 2);
    final center = Offset(size.width / 2, size.height / 2);
    Offset P(double x, double y, double z) => center + (pu(x, y, z) - bc) * scale;

    // Floor outline
    final floor = Path()
      ..moveTo(P(-cols / 2, -rows / 2, 0).dx, P(-cols / 2, -rows / 2, 0).dy)
      ..lineTo(P(cols / 2, -rows / 2, 0).dx, P(cols / 2, -rows / 2, 0).dy)
      ..lineTo(P(cols / 2, rows / 2, 0).dx, P(cols / 2, rows / 2, 0).dy)
      ..lineTo(P(-cols / 2, rows / 2, 0).dx, P(-cols / 2, rows / 2, 0).dy)
      ..close();
    canvas.drawPath(floor, Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 1
      ..color = TM.cyan.withOpacity(0.4));

    // Painter's order: far -> near (larger rx+ry is nearer to the viewer).
    final order = <List<num>>[];
    for (var r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      for (var c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
        final x = c - cols / 2 + 0.5, y = r - rows / 2 + 0.5;
        order.add([r, c, (x * cr - y * sr) + (x * sr + y * cr)]);
      }
    }
    order.sort((a, b) => a[2].compareTo(b[2]));

    void quad(List<Offset> pts, Color color, {double stroke = 0, Color? sc}) {
      final path = Path()..addPolygon(pts, true);
      canvas.drawPath(path, Paint()..color = color);
      canvas.drawPath(path, Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = stroke == 0 ? (wireframe ? 0.9 : 0.4) : stroke
        ..color = sc ?? (wireframe ? Colors.white54 : Colors.black26));
    }

    for (final o in order) {
      final r = o[0].toInt(), c = o[1].toInt();
      final v = m[r][c];
      final hid = hidden.contains(layerOf(v, stats));
      final n = ((v - lo) / span).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
      final h = hid ? 0.1 : math.max(0.15, n * maxH);
      final base = hid
          ? Colors.blueGrey.withOpacity(0.18)
          : colors.colorOf(v).withOpacity(wireframe ? 0.12 : (volumetric ? 0.4 : 1.0));
      final x = c - cols / 2, y = r - rows / 2;

      // [x1,y1,x2,y2,nx,ny] for the 4 side faces
      final sides = [
        [x, y, x + 1, y, 0.0, -1.0],
        [x + 1, y, x + 1, y + 1, 1.0, 0.0],
        [x + 1, y + 1, x, y + 1, 0.0, 1.0],
        [x, y + 1, x, y, -1.0, 0.0],
      ];
      for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        final s = sides[i];
        final nx = s[4], ny = s[5];
        final vis = (nx * cr - ny * sr) + (nx * sr + ny * cr) > 0;
        if (!vis) continue;
        quad([P(s[0], s[1], 0), P(s[2], s[3], 0), P(s[2], s[3], h), P(s[0], s[1], h)],
            Color.lerp(base, Colors.black, i.isEven ? 0.4 : 0.25)!);
      }
      final marked = highlight != null && !hid && highlight!.contains(Offset(c + 0.5, r + 0.5));
      quad([P(x, y, h), P(x + 1, y, h), P(x + 1, y + 1, h), P(x, y + 1, h)], base,
          stroke: marked ? 1.8 : 0, sc: marked ? TM.gold : null);
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant IsoGridPainter old) => true;
}
=== FILE: lib/widgets/tech_background.dart ===
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Subtle technical pattern: blueprint grid + radar arcs + survey dots.
class TechBackground extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  const TechBackground({super.key, required this.child});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Stack(fit: StackFit.expand, children: [
        const CustomPaint(painter: _TechPainter()),
        child,
      ]);
}

class _TechPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _TechPainter();
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size s) {
    final line = Paint()
      ..color = TM.cyan.withOpacity(0.05)
      ..strokeWidth = 1;
    for (double x = 0; x < s.width; x += 36) {
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(x, 0), Offset(x, s.height), line);
    }
    for (double y = 0; y < s.height; y += 36) {
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(0, y), Offset(s.width, y), line);
    }
    final arc = Paint()
      ..color = TM.cyan.withOpacity(0.07)
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 1.5;
    final c = Offset(s.width * 0.9, s.height * 0.85);
    for (var r = 60.0; r < s.width * 1.3; r += 60) {
      canvas.drawCircle(c, r, arc);
    }
    final dot = Paint()..color = TM.gold.withOpacity(0.18);
    final rnd = math.Random(3);
    for (var i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
      canvas.drawCircle(Offset(rnd.nextDouble() * s.width, rnd.nextDouble() * s.height), 2, dot);
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant CustomPainter old) => false;
}
=== FILE: lib/widgets/terramag_logo.dart ===
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Logo: navy tile, cyan isometric grid, two magnetic waves, gold detection cell.
class TerraMagLogo extends StatelessWidget {
  final double size;
  const TerraMagLogo({super.key, this.size = 56});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) =>
      CustomPaint(size: Size.square(size), painter: _LogoPainter());
}

class _LogoPainter extends CustomPainter {
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size sz) {
    final s = sz.width;
    final tile = RRect.fromRectAndRadius(Offset.zero & sz, Radius.circular(s * 0.22));
    canvas.drawRRect(tile, Paint()..color = TM.navyDeep);
    canvas.drawRRect(tile, Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = s * 0.03
      ..color = TM.cyan.withOpacity(0.6));

    final w = s * 0.30, h = s * 0.15, top = s * 0.46, cx = s / 2;
    Offset pt(double u, double v) => Offset(cx + (u - v) * w, top + (u + v) * h);
    final grid = Paint()
      ..color = TM.cyan.withOpacity(0.55)
      ..strokeWidth = s * 0.018;
    for (var i = 0; i <= 3; i++) {
      final t = i / 3;
      canvas.drawLine(pt(t, 0), pt(t, 1), grid);
      canvas.drawLine(pt(0, t), pt(1, t), grid);
    }
    final cell = Path()
      ..moveTo(pt(1 / 3, 1 / 3).dx, pt(1 / 3, 1 / 3).dy)
      ..lineTo(pt(2 / 3, 1 / 3).dx, pt(2 / 3, 1 / 3).dy)
      ..lineTo(pt(2 / 3, 2 / 3).dx, pt(2 / 3, 2 / 3).dy)
      ..lineTo(pt(1 / 3, 2 / 3).dx, pt(1 / 3, 2 / 3).dy)
      ..close();
    canvas.drawPath(cell, Paint()..color = TM.gold);

    for (var k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
      final path = Path();
      for (var i = 0; i <= 40; i++) {
        final x = s * 0.14 + (s * 0.72) * i / 40;
        final y = s * (0.24 + 0.09 * k) + s * 0.06 * math.sin(i / 40 * 2 * math.pi * 1.5 + k * 1.2);
        if (i == 0) {
          path.moveTo(x, y);
        } else {
          path.lineTo(x, y);
        }
      }
      canvas.drawPath(path, Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = s * 0.035
        ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
        ..color = TM.cyan.withOpacity(k == 0 ? 1 : 0.55));
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant CustomPainter old) => false;
}
=== FILE: lib/widgets/thermal_bar.dart ===
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Tall vertical thermal bar (Grey -> Gold -> Red). [fraction] 0..1, with scale ticks (top -> bottom).
class ThermalBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final double fraction;
  final List<String> ticks;
  const ThermalBar({super.key, required this.fraction, required this.ticks});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final t = fraction.clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
    return LayoutBuilder(builder: (_, c) {
      final h = c.maxHeight.isFinite ? c.maxHeight : 300.0;
      return SizedBox(
        height: h,
        child: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          Container(
            width: 76,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(38),
              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white24, width: 2),
              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                begin: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                end: Alignment.topCenter,
                colors: [TM.grey, TM.gold, TM.hot],
              ),
            ),
            child: Stack(children: [
              AnimatedPositioned(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
                top: 0, left: 0, right: 0,
                height: h * (1 - t),
                child: Container(
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: TM.navy.withOpacity(0.86),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(38),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ]),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Column(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [for (final l in ticks) Text(l, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: TM.grey))],
          ),
        ]),
      );
    });
  }
}
=== FILE: lib/widgets/trace_chart.dart ===
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../services/app_state.dart';

/// Live trace of the latest readings with the baseline and the detection band.
class TraceChart extends StatelessWidget {
  final double height;
  const TraceChart({super.key, this.height = 80});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = AppState.I;
    return Container(
      height: height,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: TM.navySurface.withOpacity(0.7),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        child: ListenableBuilder(
          listenable: Listenable.merge([s.trace, s.baseline, s.thresholdNt, s.ntPerCount]),
          builder: (_, __) => CustomPaint(
            size: Size.infinite,
            painter: _TracePainter(
              List<double>.from(s.trace.data),
              s.baseline.value,
              s.thresholdNt.value / s.ntPerCount.value,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _TracePainter extends CustomPainter {
  final List<double> v;
  final double? base;
  final double thr; // counts
  _TracePainter(this.v, this.base, this.thr);

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    if (v.length < 2) return;
    var lo = v.reduce(math.min), hi = v.reduce(math.max);
    if (base != null) {
      lo = math.min(lo, base! - thr);
      hi = math.max(hi, base! + thr);
    }
    final span = (hi - lo).abs() < 1e-9 ? 1.0 : hi - lo;
    double y(double val) => size.height - 6 - (val - lo) / span * (size.height - 12);
    double x(int i) => i / (v.length - 1) * size.width;

    if (base != null) {
      canvas.drawRect(Rect.fromLTRB(0, y(base! + thr), size.width, y(base! - thr)), Paint()..color = TM.cyan.withOpacity(0.10));
      canvas.drawLine(Offset(0, y(base!)), Offset(size.width, y(base!)), Paint()
        ..color = TM.grey
        ..strokeWidth = 1);
    }
    final path = Path();
    for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) {
      final p = Offset(x(i), y(v[i]));
      i == 0 ? path.moveTo(p.dx, p.dy) : path.lineTo(p.dx, p.dy);
    }
    canvas.drawPath(path, Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 2
      ..color = TM.cyan);
    canvas.drawCircle(Offset(x(v.length - 1), y(v.last)), 4, Paint()..color = TM.gold);
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _TracePainter old) => true;
}
